IPOPT is an interior-point solver for large-scale NLP.\n\n## Quick start (Linux)\n\n```bash\napt-get update -qq && apt-get install -y -qq libgfortran5\npip install numpy==1.26.4 casadi==3.6.7\n```\n\n## Building an NLP\n\n### 1. Decision variables\n\n```python\nimport casadi as ca\n\nn_bus, n_gen = 100, 20\nVm = ca.MX.sym(\"Vm\", n_bus)   # Voltage magnitudes\nVa = ca.MX.sym(\"Va\", n_bus)   # Voltage angles (radians)\nPg = ca.MX.sym(\"Pg\", n_gen)   # Real power\nQg = ca.MX.sym(\"Qg\", n_gen)   # Reactive power\n\n# Stack into single vector for solver\nx = ca.vertcat(Vm, Va, Pg, Qg)\n```\n\n### 2. Objective function\n\nBuild symbolic expression:\n\n```python\n# Quadratic cost: sum of c2*P^2 + c1*P + c0\nobj = ca.MX(0)\nfor k in range(n_gen):\n    obj += c2[k] * Pg[k]**2 + c1[k] * Pg[k] + c0[k]\n```\n\n### 3. Constraints\n\nCollect constraints in lists with bounds:\n\n```python\ng_expr = []  # Constraint expressions\nlbg = []     # Lower bounds\nubg = []     # Upper bounds\n\n# Equality constraint: g(x) = 0\ng_expr.append(some_expression)\nlbg.append(0.0)\nubg.append(0.0)\n\n# Inequality constraint: g(x) <= limit\ng_expr.append(another_expression)\nlbg.append(-ca.inf)\nubg.append(limit)\n\n# Two-sided: lo <= g(x) <= hi\ng_expr.append(bounded_expression)\nlbg.append(lo)\nubg.append(hi)\n\ng = ca.vertcat(*g_expr)\n```\n\n### 4. Variable bounds\n\n```python\n# Stack bounds matching variable order\nlbx = np.concatenate([Vm_min, Va_min, Pg_min, Qg_min]).tolist()\nubx = np.concatenate([Vm_max, Va_max, Pg_max, Qg_max]).tolist()\n```\n\n### 5. Use multiple initializations:\n\n```python\ninitializations = [x0_from_data, x0_flat_start]\nbest_sol = None\n\nfor x0 in initializations:\n    try:\n        sol = solver(x0=x0, lbx=lbx, ubx=ubx, lbg=lbg, ubg=ubg)\n        if best_sol is None or float(sol[\"f\"]) < float(best_sol[\"f\"]):\n            best_sol = sol\n    except Exception:\n        continue\n\nif best_sol is None:\n    raise RuntimeError(\"Solver failed from all initializations\")\n```\n\n**Good initialization strategies:**\n- Data-derived: Use values from input data, clipped to bounds\n- Flat start: Nominal values (e.g., Vm=1.0, Va=0.0)\n- Always enforce known constraints in initial point (e.g., reference angle = 0)\n\n## Extracting solutions\n\n```python\nx_opt = np.array(sol[\"x\"]).flatten()\n\n# Unpack by slicing (must match variable order)\nVm_sol = x_opt[:n_bus]\nVa_sol = x_opt[n_bus:2*n_bus]\nPg_sol = x_opt[2*n_bus:2*n_bus+n_gen]\nQg_sol = x_opt[2*n_bus+n_gen:]\n```\n\n## Power systems patterns\n\n### Per-unit scaling\n\nWork in per-unit internally, convert for output:\n\n```python\nbaseMVA = 100.0\nPg_pu = Pg_MW / baseMVA      # Input conversion\nPg_MW = Pg_pu * baseMVA      # Output conversion\n```\n\nCost functions often expect MW, not per-unit - Voltage max [pu]\n        self.Vmin = bus_array[:, 12]  # Voltage min [pu]\n\n        # Generators: [bus, Pg, Qg, Qmax, Qmin, Vg, mBase, status, Pmax, Pmin, ...]\n        gen_array = np.array(self.data['gen'])\n        self.n_gen = len(gen_array)\n        self.gen_bus = gen_array[:, 0].astype(int)\n        self.gen_bus_idx = [self.bus_id_to_idx[bid] for bid in self.gen_bus]\n        self.Pg_init = gen_array[:, 1]  # Initial real power [MW]\n        self.Qg_init = gen_array[:, 2]  # Initial reactive power [MVAr]\n        self.Qmax = gen_array[:, 3]  # Reactive power max [MVAr]\n        self.Qmin = gen_array[:, 4]  # Reactive power min [MVAr]\n        self.Pmax = gen_array[:, 8]  # Real power max [MW]\n        self.Pmin = gen_array[:, 9]  # Real power min [MW]\n\n        # Generator costs: [model, startup, shutdown, n, c_n-1, ..., c_0]\n        # For quadratic: [2, 0, 0, 3, c2, c1, c0]\n        cost_array = np.array(self.data['gencost'])\n        self.c2 = np.zeros(self.n_gen)\n        self.c1 = np.zeros(self.n_gen)\n        self.c0 = np.zeros(self.n_gen)\n        for k in range(self.n_gen):\n            if cost_array[k, 0] == 2:  # Quadratic cost\n                n_coeffs = int(cost_array[k, 3])\n                if n_coeffs >= 3:\n                    self.c2[k] = cost_array[k, 4]      # Coefficient of P^2\n                    self.c1[k] = cost_array[k, 5]      # Coefficient of P\n                    self.c0[k] = cost_array[k, 6]      # Constant\n\n        # Branches: [from, to, r, x, b, rateA, rateB, rateC, ratio, angle, status, ...]\n        branch_array = np.array(self.data['branch'])\n        self.n_branch = len(branch_array)\n        self.from_bus = branch_array[:, 0].astype(int)\n        self.to_bus = branch_array[:, 1].astype(int)\n        self.from_bus_idx = [self.bus_id_to_idx[bid] for bid in self.from_bus]\n        self.to_bus_idx = [self.bus_id_to_idx[bid] for bid in self.to_bus]\n        self.branch_r = branch_array[:, 2]  # Resistance [pu]\n        self.branch_x = branch_array[:, 3]  # Reactance [pu]\n        self.branch_b = branch_array[:, 4]  # Shunt susceptance [pu]\n        self.branch_limit = branch_array[:, 5]  # MVA limit (rateA)\n        self.branch_status = branch_array[:, 10].astype(int)  # 1=in service, 0=out\n\n        # Complex admittance\n        self.branch_y = 1.0 / (self.branch_r + 1j * self.branch_x)\n\n        # Find reference bus (slack bus, type 3)\n        self.ref_bus_idx = np.where(self.bus_type == 3)[0]\n        if len(self.ref_bus_idx) == 0:\n            # Fall back to first PV bus\n            pv_idx = np.where(self.bus_type >= 2)[0]\n            self.ref_bus_idx = pv_idx if len(pv_idx) > 0 else np.array([0])\n        self.ref_bus_idx = self.ref_bus_idx[0]\n\n    def build_nlp(self) -> Tuple[Dict, Dict, np.ndarray, np.ndarray]:\n        \"\"\"Build the NLP problem for AC OPF\"\"\"\n\n        # Decision variables\n        Vm = ca.MX.sym(\"Vm\", self.n_bus)  # Voltage magnitude [pu]\n        Va = ca.MX.sym(\"Va\", self.n_bus)  # Voltage angle [rad]\n        Pg = ca.MX.sym(\"Pg\", self.n_gen)  # Real power generation [MW]\n        Qg = ca.MX.sym(\"Qg\", self.n_gen)  # Reactive power generation [MVAr]\n\n        x = ca.vertcat(Vm, Va, Pg, Qg)\n        n_var = self.n_bus * 2 + self.n_gen * 2\n\n        # Objective: minimize generation cost\n        obj = ca.MX(0)\n        for k in range(self.n_gen):\n            obj += self.c2[k] * Pg[k]**2 + self.c1[k] * Pg[k] + self.c0[k]\n\n        # Constraints\n        g_expr = []\n        lbg = []\n        ubg = []\n\n        # 1. Reference bus angle constraint: Va[ref] = 0\n        g_expr.append(Va[self.ref_bus_idx])\n        lbg.append(0.0)\n        ubg.append(0.0)\n\n        # 2. Power balance constraints (AC power flow equations)\n        # For each bus: P_gen - P_load - P_shunt = sum of P_flows\n        #              Q_gen - Q_load - Q_shunt = sum of Q_flows\n\n        for i in range(self.n_bus):\n            # Collect generation at bus i\n            P_gen_i = ca.MX(0)\n            Q_gen_i = ca.MX(0)\n            for k in range(self.n_gen):\n                if self.gen_bus_idx[k] == i:\n                    P_gen_i += Pg[k]\n                    Q_gen_i += Qg[k]\n\n            # Load and shunt\n            P_load_i = self.Pd[i]\n            Q_load_i = self.Qd[i]\n            P_shunt_i = self.Gs[i] * Vm[i]**2\n            Q_shunt_i = -self.Bs[i] * Vm[i]**2\n\n            # Calculate power flows from all branches connected to bus i\n            P_flow_i = ca.MX(0)\n            Q_flow_i = ca.MX(0)\n\n            for j in range(self.n_branch):\n                if self.branch_status[j] == 0:\n                    continue\n\n                # Branch admittance (series)\n                y_ij = self.branch_y[j]\n                g_ij = y_ij.real\n                b_ij = y_ij.imag\n\n                # Shunt admittance at each end\n                b_shunt_from = self.branch_b[j] / 2.0\n                b_shunt_to = self.branch_b[j] / 2.0\n\n                # Branch power flow (from i to j)\n                if self.from_bus_idx[j] == i:\n                    j_idx = self.to_bus_idx[j]\n                    # P_ij = Vm[i]^2 * g_ij - Vm[i]*Vm[j] * (g_ij*cos(θ_ij) + b_ij*sin(θ_ij))\n                    # Q_ij = -Vm[i]^2 * (b_ij + b_shunt_from) + Vm[i]*Vm[j] * (g_ij*sin(θ_ij) - b_ij*cos(θ_ij))\n                    theta_ij = Va[i] - Va[j_idx]\n                    P_ij = Vm[i]**2 * g_ij - Vm[i] * Vm[j_idx] * (g_ij * ca.cos(theta_ij) + b_ij * ca.sin(theta_ij))\n                    Q_ij = -Vm[i]**2 * (b_ij + b_shunt_from) + Vm[i] * Vm[j_idx] * (g_ij * ca.sin(theta_ij) - b_ij * ca.cos(theta_ij))\n                    P_flow_i += P_ij\n                    Q_flow_i += Q_ij\n\n                # Branch power flow (to i from j)\n                elif self.to_bus_idx[j] == i:\n                    j_idx = self.from_bus_idx[j]\n                    # P_ji = Vm[i]^2 * g_ij - Vm[i]*Vm[j] * (g_ij*cos(θ_ji) + b_ij*sin(θ_ji))\n                    theta_ji = Va[i] - Va[j_idx]\n                    P_ji = Vm[i]**2 * g_ij - Vm[i] * Vm[j_idx] * (g_ij * ca.cos(theta_ji) + b_ij * ca.sin(theta_ji))\n                    Q_ji = -Vm[i]**2 * (b_ij + b_shunt_to) + Vm[i] * Vm[j_idx] * (g_ij * ca.sin(theta_ji) - b_ij * ca.cos(theta_ji))\n                    P_flow_i += P_ji\n                    Q_flow_i += Q_ji\n\n            # Power balance: injection = flow\n            # P: P_gen - P_load - P_shunt = P_flow\n            g_expr.append(P_gen_i - P_load_i - P_shunt_i - P_flow_i)\n            lbg.append(0.0)\n            ubg.append(0.0)\n\n            # Q: Q_gen - Q_load - Q_shunt = Q_flow\n            g_expr.append(Q_gen_i - Q_load_i - Q_shunt_i - Q_flow_i)\n            lbg.append(0.0)\n            ubg.append(0.0)\n\n        # 3. Branch MVA limit constraints\n        for j in range(self.n_branch):\n            if self.branch_status[j] == 0:\n                continue\n\n            # Simplified: |S_ij| <= limit\n            y_ij = self.branch_y[j]\n            g_ij = y_ij.real\n            b_ij = y_ij.imag\n\n            from_idx = self.from_bus_idx[j]\n            to_idx = self.to_bus_idx[j]\n\n            # Power flow from i to j\n            theta_ij = Va[from_idx] - Va[to_idx]\n            P_ij = Vm[from_idx]**2 * g_ij - Vm[from_idx] * Vm[to_idx] * (g_ij * ca.cos(theta_ij) + b_ij * ca.sin(theta_ij))\n            Q_ij = -Vm[from_idx]**2 * (b_ij + self.branch_b[j]/2.0) + Vm[from_idx] * Vm[to_idx] * (g_ij * ca.sin(theta_ij) - b_ij * ca.cos(theta_ij))\n            S_ij = ca.sqrt(P_ij**2 + Q_ij**2)\n\n            g_expr.append(S_ij)\n            lbg.append(-ca.inf)\n            ubg.append(self.branch_limit[j])\n\n            # Power flow from j to i\n            theta_ji = Va[to_idx] - Va[from_idx]\n            P_ji = Vm[to_idx]**2 * g_ij - Vm[to_idx] * Vm[from_idx] * (g_ij * ca.cos(theta_ji) + b_ij * ca.sin(theta_ji))\n            Q_ji = -Vm[to_idx]**2 * (b_ij + self.branch_b[j]/2.0) + Vm[to_idx] * Vm[from_idx] * (g_ij * ca.sin(theta_ji) - b_ij * ca.cos(theta_ji))\n            S_ji = ca.sqrt(P_ji**2 + Q_ji**2)\n\n            g_expr.append(S_ji)\n            lbg.append(-ca.inf)\n            ubg.append(self.branch_limit[j])\n\n        # Stack all constraints\n        g = ca.vertcat(*g_expr)\n\n        # Variable bounds\n        lbx = []\n        ubx = []\n\n        # Voltage magnitude bounds\n        for i in range(self.n_bus):\n            lbx.append(self.Vmin[i])\n            ubx.append(self.Vmax[i])\n\n        # Voltage angle bounds\n        for i in range(self.n_bus):\n            lbx.append(-np.pi)\n            ubx.append(np.pi)\n\n        # Real power generation bounds\n        for k in range(self.n_gen):\n            lbx.append(self.Pmin[k])\n            ubx.append(self.Pmax[k])\n\n        # Reactive power generation bounds\n        for k in range(self.n_gen):\n            lbx.append(self.Qmin[k])\n            ubx.append(self.Qmax[k])\n\n        return {\"x\": x, \"f\": obj, \"g\": g}, {\"lbx\": lbx, \"ubx\": ubx, \"lbg\": lbg, \"ubg\": ubg}, Vm, Va\n\n    def solve(self) -> Dict:\n        \"\"\"Solve AC OPF and return results\"\"\"\n\n Buses: [bus_i, type, Pd, Qd, Gs, Bs, area, Vm, Va, baseKV, zone, Vmax, Vmin]\n        bus_array = np.array(self.data['bus'])\n        self.n_bus = len(bus_array)\n        self.bus_ids = bus_array[:, 0].astype(int)\n        self.bus_id_to_idx = {int(bid): i for i, bid in enumerate(self.bus_ids)}\n        self.bus_type = bus_array[:, 1].astype(int)  # 1=PQ, 2=PV, 3=Slack\n        self.Pd = bus_array[:, 2]  # Real power demand [MW]\n        self.Qd = bus_array[:, 3]  # Reactive power demand [MVAr]\n        self.Gs = bus_array[:, 4]  # Shunt conductance [pu]\n        self.Bs = bus_array[:, 5]  # Shunt susceptance [pu]\n        self.Vm_init = bus_array[:, 7]  # Initial voltage magnitude [pu]\n        self.Va_init = np.radians(bus_array[:, 8])  # Initial voltage angle [rad]\n        self.Vmax = bus_array[:, 11]  # Quadratic cost\n                n_coeffs = int(cost_array[k, 3])\n                if n_coeffs >= 3:\n                    self.c2[k] = cost_array[k, 4]      # Coefficient of P^2\n                    self.c1[k] = cost_array[k, 5]      # Coefficient of P\n                    self.c0[k] = cost_array[k, 6]      # Constant\n\n        # Branches: [from, to, r, x, b, rateA, rateB, rateC, ratio, angle, status, ...]\n        branch_array = np.array(self.data['branch'])\n        self.n_branch = len(branch_array)\n        self.from_bus = branch_array[:, 0].astype(int)\n        self.to_bus = branch_array[:, 1].astype(int)\n        self.from_bus_idx = [self.bus_id_to_idx[bid] for bid in self.from_bus]\n        self.to_bus_idx = [self.bus_id_to_idx[bid] for bid in self.to_bus]\n        self.branch_r = branch_array[:, 2]  # Resistance [pu]\n        self.branch_x = branch_array[:, 3]  # Reactance [pu]\n        self.branch_b = branch_array[:, 4]  # Shunt susceptance [pu]\n        self.branch_limit = branch_array[:, 5]  # MVA limit (rateA)\n        self.branch_status = branch_array[:, 10].astype(int)  # 1=in service, 0=out\n\n        # Complex admittance\n        self.branch_y = 1.0 / (self.branch_r + 1j * self.branch_x)\n\n        # Find reference bus (slack bus, type 3)\n        self.ref_bus_idx = np.where(self.bus_type == 3)[0]\n        if len(self.ref_bus_idx) == 0:\n            # Decision variables\n        Vm = ca.MX.sym(\"Vm\", self.n_bus)  # Voltage magnitude [pu]\n        Va = ca.MX.sym(\"Va\", self.n_bus)  # Voltage angle [rad]\n        Pg = ca.MX.sym(\"Pg\", self.n_gen)  # Real power generation [MW]\n        Qg = ca.MX.sym(\"Qg\", self.n_gen)  # Reactive power generation [MVAr]\n\n        x = ca.vertcat(Vm, Va, Pg, Qg)\n        n_var = self.n_bus * 2 + self.n_gen * 2\n\n        # Objective: minimize generation cost\n        obj = ca.MX(0)\n        for k in range(self.n_gen):\n            obj += self.c2[k] * Pg[k]**2 + self.c1[k] * Pg[k] + self.c0[k]\n\n        # Constraints\n        g_expr = []\n        lbg = []\n        ubg = []\n\n        # 1. Reference bus angle constraint: Va[ref] = 0\n        g_expr.append(Va[self.ref_bus_idx])\n        lbg.append(0.0)\n        ubg.append(0.0)\n\n        # 2. Vm[i]*Vm[j] * (g_ij*cos(θ_ij) + b_ij*sin(θ_ij))\n                    # Q_ij = -Vm[i]^2 * (b_ij + b_shunt_from) + Vm[i]*Vm[j] * (g_ij*sin(θ_ij) - b_ij*cos(θ_ij))\n                    theta_ij = Va[i] - Vm[i]*Vm[j] * (g_ij*cos(θ_ji) + b_ij*sin(θ_ji))\n                    theta_ji = Va[i] - Va[j_idx]\n                    P_ji = Vm[i]**2 * g_ij - Vm[i] * Vm[j_idx] * (g_ij * ca.cos(theta_ji) + b_ij * ca.sin(theta_ji))\n                    Q_ji = -Vm[i]**2 * (b_ij + b_shunt_to) + Vm[i] * Vm[j_idx] * (g_ij * ca.sin(theta_ji) - b_ij * ca.cos(theta_ji))\n                    P_flow_i += P_ji\n                    Q_flow_i += Q_ji\n\n            # Power balance: injection = flow\n            # P: P_gen - P_load - P_shunt = P_flow\n            g_expr.append(P_gen_i - P_load_i - P_shunt_i - P_flow_i)\n            lbg.append(0.0)\n            ubg.append(0.0)\n\n            # Q: Q_gen - Q_load - Q_shunt = Q_flow\n            g_expr.append(Q_gen_i - Q_load_i - Q_shunt_i - Branch MVA limit constraints\n        for j in range(self.n_branch):\n            if self.branch_status[j] == 0:\n                continue\n\n            # Simplified: |S_ij| <= limit\n from i to j\n            theta_ij = Va[from_idx] - Va[to_idx]\n            P_ij = Vm[from_idx]**2 * g_ij - Vm[from_idx] * Vm[to_idx] * (g_ij * ca.cos(theta_ij) + b_ij * ca.sin(theta_ij))\n            Q_ij = -Vm[from_idx]**2 * (b_ij + self.branch_b[j]/2.0) + Vm[from_idx] * Vm[to_idx] * (g_ij * ca.sin(theta_ij) - b_ij * ca.cos(theta_ij))\n            S_ij = ca.sqrt(P_ij**2 + Q_ij**2)\n\n ubg.append(self.branch_limit[j])\n\n        # Stack all constraints\n        g = ca.vertcat(*g_expr)\n\n        # Variable bounds\n        lbx = []\n        ubx = []\n\n        # Voltage magnitude bounds\n        for i in range(self.n_bus):\n            lbx.append(self.Vmin[i])\n            ubx.append(self.Vmax[i])\n\n        # Voltage angle bounds\n        for i in range(self.n_bus):\n \"\"\"Initialize solver with network data\"\"\"\n        with open(network_file, 'r') as f:\n            self.data = json.load(f)\n\n        self.baseMVA = self.data['baseMVA']\n        self.parse_network_data()\n\n    def parse_network_data(self):\n        \"\"\"Parse bus, generator, branch, and cost data\"\"\"\n        # Buses: [bus_i, type, Pd, Qd, Gs, Bs, area, Vm, Va, baseKV, zone, Vmax, Vmin]\n        bus_array = np.array(self.data['bus'])\n        self.n_bus = len(bus_array)\n        self.bus_ids = bus_array[:, 0].astype(int)\n        self.bus_id_to_idx = {int(bid): i for i, bid in enumerate(self.bus_ids)}\n        self.bus_type = bus_array[:, 1].astype(int)  # 1=PQ, 2=PV, 3=Slack\n        self.Pd = bus_array[:, 2]  # Real power demand [MW]\n        self.Qd = bus_array[:, 3]  # Reactive power demand [MVAr]\n        self.Gs = bus_array[:, 4]  # Shunt conductance [pu]\n        self.Bs = bus_array[:, 5]  # Shunt susceptance [pu]\n        self.Vm_init = bus_array[:, 7]  # Initial voltage magnitude [pu]\n        self.Va_init = np.radians(bus_array[:, 8])  # Initial voltage angle [rad]\n        self.Vmax = bus_array[:, 11]  # Voltage max [pu]\n        self.Vmin = bus_array[:, 12]  # Voltage min [pu]\n\n        # Generators: [bus, Pg, Qg, Qmax, Qmin, Vg, mBase, status, Pmax, Pmin, ...]\n        gen_array = np.array(self.data['gen'])\n        self.n_gen = len(gen_array)\n        self.gen_bus = gen_array[:, 0].astype(int)\n        self.gen_bus_idx = [self.bus_id_to_idx[bid] for bid in self.gen_bus]\n        self.Pg_init = gen_array[:, 1]  # # Reactive power min [MVAr]\n        self.Pmax = gen_array[:, 8]  # Real power max [MW]\n        self.Pmin = gen_array[:, 9]  # Real power min [MW]\n\n        # Generator costs: [model, startup, self.from_bus_idx = [self.bus_id_to_idx[bid] for bid in self.from_bus]\n        self.to_bus_idx = [self.bus_id_to_idx[bid] for bid in self.to_bus]\n        self.branch_r = branch_array[:, 2]  # Resistance [pu]\n        self.branch_x = branch_array[:, 3]  # Reactance [pu]\n        self.branch_b = branch_array[:, 4]  # Shunt susceptance [pu]\n        self.branch_limit = branch_array[:, 5]  # MVA limit (rateA)\n        self.branch_status = branch_array[:, 10].astype(int)  # 1=in service, 0=out\n\n        # Complex admittance\n        self.branch_y = 1.0 / (self.branch_r + 1j * self.branch_x)\n\n        # Find reference bus (slack bus, type 3)\n        self.ref_bus_idx = np.where(self.bus_type == 3)[0]\n        if len(self.ref_bus_idx) == 0:\n            # Fall back to first build_nlp(self) -> Tuple[Dict, Dict, np.ndarray, np.ndarray]:\n        \"\"\"Build the NLP problem for AC OPF\"\"\"\n\n        # Decision variables\n        Vm = ca.MX.sym(\"Vm\", self.n_bus)  # self.n_gen)  # Reactive power generation [MVAr]\n\n        x = ca.vertcat(Vm, Va, Pg, Qg)\n        n_var = self.n_bus * 2 + self.n_gen * 2\n\n        # Objective: minimize generation cost\n        obj = ca.MX(0)\n        for k in range(self.n_gen):\n            obj += self.c2[k] * Pg[k]**2 + self.c1[k] * Pg[k] + self.c0[k]\n\n        # Constraints\n        g_expr = []\n        lbg = []\n        ubg = []\n\n        # 1. Reference bus angle constraint: Va[ref] = 0\n        g_expr.append(Va[self.ref_bus_idx])\n        lbg.append(0.0)\n        ubg.append(0.0)\n\n        # 2. Power balance constraints (AC power flow equations)\n        # For each bus: P_gen - P_load - P_shunt = sum of P_flows\n        #              Q_gen - Q_load - Q_shunt = sum of Q_flows\n\n        for i in range(self.n_bus):\n            # Collect generation at bus i\n            P_gen_i = ca.MX(0)\n            Q_gen_i = ca.MX(0)\n            for k in range(self.n_gen):\n                if self.gen_bus_idx[k] == i:\n                    P_gen_i += Pg[k]\n                    Q_gen_i += Qg[k]\n\n            # Load and shunt\n            P_load_i = self.Pd[i]\n            Q_load_i = self.Qd[i]\n            P_shunt_i = self.Gs[i] * Vm[i]**2\n            Q_shunt_i = -self.Bs[i] * Vm[i]**2\n\n            # Calculate power flows from all branches connected to bus i\n            P_flow_i = ca.MX(0)\n            Q_flow_i = ca.MX(0)\n\n            for j in range(self.n_branch):\n                if self.branch_status[j] == 0:\n                    continue\n\n                # Branch admittance (series)\n                y_ij = self.branch_y[j]\n                g_ij = y_ij.real\n                b_ij = y_ij.imag\n\n                # Shunt admittance at each end\n                b_shunt_from = self.branch_b[j] / 2.0\n                b_shunt_to = self.branch_b[j] / 2.0\n\n                # Branch power flow (from i to j)\n                if self.from_bus_idx[j] == i:\n                    j_idx = self.to_bus_idx[j]\n                    # P_ij = Vm[i]^2 * g_ij - Vm[i]*Vm[j] * (g_ij*cos(θ_ij) + b_ij*sin(θ_ij))\n                    # Q_ij = -Vm[i]^2 * (b_ij + b_shunt_from) + Vm[i]*Vm[j] * (g_ij*sin(θ_ij) - b_ij*cos(θ_ij))\n                    theta_ij = Va[i] - Va[j_idx]\n                    P_ij = Vm[i]**2 * g_ij - Vm[i] * Vm[j_idx] * (g_ij * ca.cos(theta_ij) + b_ij * ca.sin(theta_ij))\n                    Q_ij = -Vm[i]**2 * (b_ij + b_shunt_from) + Vm[i] * Vm[j_idx] * (g_ij * ca.sin(theta_ij) - Vm[i]*Vm[j] * (g_ij*cos(θ_ji) + b_ij*sin(θ_ji))\n                    theta_ji = Va[i] - Va[j_idx]\n                    P_ji = Vm[i]**2 * g_ij - Vm[i] * Vm[j_idx] * (g_ij * ca.cos(theta_ji) + b_ij * ca.sin(theta_ji))\n                    Q_ji = -Vm[i]**2 * (b_ij + b_shunt_to) + Vm[i] * Vm[j_idx] * (g_ij * ca.sin(theta_ji) - P_load_i - P_shunt_i - P_flow_i)\n            lbg.append(0.0)\n            ubg.append(0.0)\n\n            # Q: Q_gen - Q_load - Q_shunt = Q_flow\n            g_expr.append(Q_gen_i - Q_load_i - Q_shunt_i - y_ij = self.branch_y[j]\n            g_ij = y_ij.real\n            b_ij = y_ij.imag\n\n            from_idx = self.from_bus_idx[j]\n            to_idx = self.to_bus_idx[j]\n\n            # Power flow Q_ij = -Vm[from_idx]**2 * (b_ij + self.branch_b[j]/2.0) + Vm[from_idx] * Vm[to_idx] * (g_ij * ca.sin(theta_ij) - b_ij * ca.cos(theta_ij))\n            S_ij = ca.sqrt(P_ij**2 + Q_ij**2)\n\n            g_expr.append(S_ij)\n            lbg.append(-ca.inf)\n            ubg.append(self.branch_limit[j])\n\n            # Power flow from j to i\n            theta_ji = Va[to_idx] - Va[from_idx]\n {"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"type":"text","text":"Base directory for this skill: /logs/agent/sessions/skills/ac-branch-pi-model\n\n# AC Branch Pi-Model + Transformer Handling\n\nImplement the exact branch power flow equations in `acopf-math-model.md` using MATPOWER branch data:\n\n```\n[F_BUS, T_BUS, BR_R, BR_X, BR_B, RATE_A, RATE_B, RATE_C, TAP, SHIFT, BR_STATUS, ANGMIN, ANGMAX]\n```\n\n## Quick start\n\n- Use `scripts/branch_flows.py` to compute per-unit branch flows.\n- Treat the results as **power leaving** the “from” bus and **power leaving** the “to” bus (i.e., compute both directions explicitly).\n\nExample:\n\n```python\nimport json\nimport numpy as np\n\nfrom scripts.branch_flows import compute_branch_flows_pu, build_bus_id_to_idx\n\ndata = json.load(open(\"/root/network.json\"))\nbaseMVA = float(data[\"baseMVA\"])\nbuses = np.array(data[\"bus\"], dtype=float)\nbranches = np.array(data[\"branch\"], dtype=float)\n\nbus_id_to_idx = build_bus_id_to_idx(buses)\n\nVm = buses[:, 7]  # initial guess VM\nVa = np.deg2rad(buses[:, 8])  # initial guess VA\n\nbr = branches[0]\nP_ij, Q_ij, P_ji, Q_ji = compute_branch_flows_pu(Vm, Va, br, bus_id_to_idx)\n\nS_ij_MVA = (P_ij**2 + Q_ij**2) ** 0.5 * baseMVA\nS_ji_MVA = (P_ji**2 + Q_ji**2) ** 0.5 * baseMVA\nprint(S_ij_MVA, S_ji_MVA)\n```\n\n## Model details (match the task formulation)\n\n### Per-unit conventions\n\n- Work in per-unit internally.\n- Convert with `baseMVA`:\n  - \\(P_{pu} = P_{MW} / baseMVA\\)\n  - \\(Q_{pu} = Q_{MVAr} / baseMVA\\)\n  - \\(|S|_{MVA} = |S|_{pu} \\cdot baseMVA\\)\n\n### Transformer handling (MATPOWER TAP + SHIFT)\n\n- Use \\(T_{ij} = tap \\cdot e^{j \\cdot shift}\\).\n- **Implementation shortcut (real tap + phase shift):**\n  - If `abs(TAP) < 1e-12`, treat `tap = 1.0` (no transformer).\n  - Convert `SHIFT` from degrees to radians.\n  - Use the angle shift by modifying the angle difference:\n    - \\(\\delta_{ij} = \\theta_i - \\theta_j - shift\\)\n    - \\(\\delta_{ji} = \\theta_j - \\theta_i + shift\\)\n\n### Series admittance\n\nGiven `BR_R = r`, `BR_X = x`:\n\n- If `r == 0 and x == 0`, set `g = 0`, `b = 0` (avoid divide-by-zero).\n- Else:\n  - \\(y = 1/(r + jx) = g + jb\\)\n  - \\(g = r/(r^2 + x^2)\\)\n  - \\(b = -x/(r^2 + x^2)\\)\n\n### Line charging susceptance\n\n- `BR_B` is the **total** line charging susceptance \\(b_c\\) (per unit).\n- Each end gets \\(b_c/2\\) in the standard pi model.\n\n## Power flow equations (use these exactly)\n\nLet:\n- \\(V_i = |V_i| e^{j\\theta_i}\\), \\(V_j = |V_j| e^{j\\theta_j}\\)\n- `tap` is real, `shift` is radians\n- `inv_t = 1/tap`, `inv_t2 = inv_t^2`\n\nThen the real/reactive power flow from i→j is:\n\n- \\(P_{ij} = g |V_i|^2 inv\\_t2 - |V_i||V_j| inv\\_t (g\\cos\\delta_{ij} + b\\sin\\delta_{ij})\\)\n- \\(Q_{ij} = -(b + b_c/2)|V_i|^2 inv\\_t2 - |V_i||V_j| inv\\_t (g\\sin\\delta_{ij} - b\\cos\\delta_{ij})\\)\n\nAnd from j→i is:\n\n- \\(P_{ji} = g |V_j|^2 - |V_i||V_j| inv\\_t (g\\cos\\delta_{ji} + b\\sin\\delta_{ji})\\)\n- \\(Q_{ji} = -(b + b_c/2)|V_j|^2 - |V_i||V_j| inv\\_t (g\\sin\\delta_{ji} - b\\cos\\delta_{ji})\\)\n\nCompute apparent power:\n\n- \\(|S_{ij}| = \\sqrt{P_{ij}^2 + Q_{ij}^2}\\)\n- \\(|S_{ji}| = \\sqrt{P_{ji}^2 + Q_{ji}^2}\\)\n\n## Common uses\n\n### Enforce MVA limits (rateA)\n\n- `RATE_A` is an MVA limit (may be 0 meaning “no limit”).\n- Enforce in both directions:\n  - \\(|S_{ij}| \\le RATE_A\\)\n  - \\(|S_{ji}| \\le RATE_A\\)\n\n### Compute branch loading %\n\nFor reporting “most loaded branches”:\n\n- `loading_pct = 100 * max(|S_ij|, |S_ji|) / RATE_A` if `RATE_A > 0`, else 0.\n\n### Aggregate bus injections for nodal balance\n\nTo build the branch flow sum for each bus \\(i\\):\n\n- Add \\(P_{ij}, Q_{ij}\\) to bus i\n- Add \\(P_{ji}, Q_{ji}\\) to bus j\n\nThis yields arrays `P_out[i]`, `Q_out[i]` such that the nodal balance can be written as:\n\n- \\(P^g - P^d - G^s|V|^2 = P_{out}\\)\n- \\(Q^g - Q^d + B^s|V|^2 = Q_{out}\\)\n\n## Sanity checks (fast debug)\n\n- With `SHIFT=0` and `TAP=1`, if \\(V_i = V_j\\) and \\(\\theta_i=\\theta_j\\), then \\(P_{ij}\\approx 0\\) and \\(P_{ji}\\approx 0\\) (lossless only if r=0).\n- For a pure transformer (`r=x=0`) you should not get meaningful flows; treat as `g=b=0` (no series element).\n"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"94be2023-f34d-4bb1-a5ac-3098fca0d039","uuid":"db45685f-5f76-4488-a3a6-8b08f5c65847","isSynthetic":true}

{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-haiku-4-5-20251001","id":"msg_vrtx_01S5jNuogQ3zqn1m5C65d5VR","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_vrtx_011Mzr6ZHZ9vDG7DbPRPBM2F","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/root/ac_opf_solver.py","content":"#!/usr/bin/env python3\n\"\"\"\nAC Optimal Power Flow Solver for ISO Day-Ahead Base Case\nUses CasADi + IPOPT with proper AC branch π-model equations\n\"\"\"\n\nimport json\nimport numpy as np\nimport casadi as ca\nfrom typing import Dict, List, Tuple\nimport sys\n\n\nclass ACOPFSolver:\n    def __init__(self, network_file: str):\n        \"\"\"Initialize solver with network data\"\"\"\n        with open(network_file, 'r') as f:\n            self.data = json.load(f)\n\n        self.baseMVA = self.data['baseMVA']\n        self.parse_network_data()\n\n    def parse_network_data(self):\n        \"\"\"Parse bus, generator, branch, and cost data\"\"\"\n        # Buses: [bus_i, type, Pd, Qd, Gs, Bs, area, Vm, Va, baseKV, zone, Vmax, Vmin]\n        bus_array = np.array(self.data['bus'])\n        self.n_bus = len(bus_array)\n        self.bus_ids = bus_array[:, 0].astype(int)\n        self.bus_id_to_idx = {int(bid): i for i, bid in enumerate(self.bus_ids)}\n        self.bus_type = bus_array[:, 1].astype(int)  # 1=PQ, 2=PV, 3=Slack\n        self.Pd = bus_array[:, 2]  # Real power demand [MW]\n        self.Qd = bus_array[:, 3]  # Reactive power demand [MVAr]\n        self.Gs = bus_array[:, 4]  # Shunt conductance [pu]\n        self.Bs = bus_array[:, 5]  # Shunt susceptance [pu]\n        self.Vm_init = bus_array[:, 7]  # Initial voltage magnitude [pu]\n        self.Va_init = np.radians(bus_array[:, 8])  # Initial voltage angle [rad]\n        self.Vmax = bus_array[:, 11]  # Voltage max [pu]\n        self.Vmin = bus_array[:, 12]  # Voltage min [pu]\n\n        # Generators: [bus, Pg, Qg, Qmax, Qmin, Vg, mBase, status, Pmax, Pmin, ...]\n        gen_array = np.array(self.data['gen'])\n        self.n_gen = len(gen_array)\n        self.gen_bus = gen_array[:, 0].astype(int)\n        self.gen_bus_idx = [self.bus_id_to_idx[bid] for bid in self.gen_bus]\n        self.Pg_init = gen_array[:, 1]  # Initial real power [MW]\n        self.Qg_init = gen_array[:, 2]  # Initial reactive power [MVAr]\n        self.Qmax = gen_array[:, 3]  # Reactive power max [MVAr]\n        self.Qmin = gen_array[:, 4]  # Reactive power min [MVAr]\n        self.Pmax = gen_array[:, 8]  # Real power max [MW]\n        self.Pmin = gen_array[:, 9]  # Real power min [MW]\n\n        # Generator costs: [model, startup, shutdown, n, c_n-1, ..., c_0]\n        cost_array = np.array(self.data['gencost'])\n        self.c2 = np.zeros(self.n_gen)\n        self.c1 = np.zeros(self.n_gen)\n        self.c0 = np.zeros(self.n_gen)\n        for k in range(self.n_gen):\n            if cost_array[k, 0] == 2:  # Quadratic cost\n                n_coeffs = int(cost_array[k, 3])\n                if n_coeffs >= 3:\n                    self.c2[k] = cost_array[k, 4]      # Coefficient of P^2\n                    self.c1[k] = cost_array[k, 5]      # Coefficient of P\n                    self.c0[k] = cost_array[k, 6]      # Constant\n\n        # Branches: [from, to, r, x, b, rateA, rateB, rateC, tap, angle, status, ...]\n        branch_array = np.array(self.data['branch'])\n        self.n_branch = len(branch_array)\n        self.from_bus = branch_array[:, 0].astype(int)\n        self.to_bus = branch_array[:, 1].astype(int)\n        self.from_bus_idx = [self.bus_id_to_idx[bid] for bid in self.from_bus]\n        self.to_bus_idx = [self.bus_id_to_idx[bid] for bid in self.to_bus]\n        self.branch_r = branch_array[:, 2]  # Resistance [pu]\n        self.branch_x = branch_array[:, 3]  # Reactance [pu]\n        self.branch_b = branch_array[:, 4]  # Shunt susceptance [pu]\n        self.branch_limit = branch_array[:, 5]  # MVA limit (rateA)\n        self.branch_tap = branch_array[:, 8]  # Transformer tap ratio\n        self.branch_shift = np.radians(branch_array[:, 9])  # Phase shift [rad]\n        self.branch_status = branch_array[:, 10].astype(int)  # 1=in service\n\n        # Compute branch admittances with proper handling of tap/shift\n        self.branch_y = np.zeros(self.n_branch, dtype=complex)\n        self.branch_tap_eff = np.ones(self.n_branch)\n        self.branch_shift_eff = np.zeros(self.n_branch)\n\n        for j in range(self.n_branch):\n            r, x = self.branch_r[j], self.branch_x[j]\n            if abs(r) < 1e-12 and abs(x) < 1e-12:\n                self.branch_y[j] = 0\n            else:\n                self.branch_y[j] = 1.0 / (r + 1j * x)\n\n            # Handle transformer tap and shift\n            tap = self.branch_tap[j]\n            if abs(tap) < 1e-12:\n                tap = 1.0\n            self.branch_tap_eff[j] = tap\n            self.branch_shift_eff[j] = self.branch_shift[j]\n\n        # Find reference bus (slack bus, type 3)\n        self.ref_bus_idx = np.where(self.bus_type == 3)[0]\n        if len(self.ref_bus_idx) == 0:\n            # Fall back to first PV bus\n            pv_idx = np.where(self.bus_type >= 2)[0]\n            self.ref_bus_idx = pv_idx if len(pv_idx) > 0 else np.array([0])\n        self.ref_bus_idx = self.ref_bus_idx[0]\n\n    def compute_branch_flow_expressions(self, Vm: ca.MX, Va: ca.MX, j: int) -> Tuple:\n        \"\"\"\n        Compute AC branch π-model power flows for branch j\n        Returns: (P_ij, Q_ij, P_ji, Q_ji)\n        \"\"\"\n        from_idx = self.from_bus_idx[j]\n        to_idx = self.to_bus_idx[j]\n\n        # Series admittance\n        y_ij = self.branch_y[j]\n        g_ij = y_ij.real\n        b_ij = y_ij.imag\n\n        # Shunt admittance\n        b_shunt = self.branch_b[j] / 2.0\n\n        # Transformer parameters\n        tap = self.branch_tap_eff[j]\n        shift = self.branch_shift_eff[j]\n        inv_tap = 1.0 / tap\n        inv_tap2 = inv_tap ** 2\n\n        # Voltages\n        Vi = Vm[from_idx]\n        Vj = Vm[to_idx]\n        thetai = Va[from_idx]\n        thetaj = Va[to_idx]\n\n        # Angle differences with transformer shift\n        delta_ij = thetai - thetaj - shift\n        delta_ji = thetaj - thetai + shift\n\n        # Power flow from i to j (with transformer on i side)\n        P_ij = (g_ij * Vi**2 * inv_tap2\n                - Vi * Vj * inv_tap * (g_ij * ca.cos(delta_ij) + b_ij * ca.sin(delta_ij)))\n        Q_ij = (-(b_ij + b_shunt) * Vi**2 * inv_tap2\n                - Vi * Vj * inv_tap * (g_ij * ca.sin(delta_ij) - b_ij * ca.cos(delta_ij)))\n\n        # Power flow from j to i\n        P_ji = (g_ij * Vj**2\n                - Vi * Vj * inv_tap * (g_ij * ca.cos(delta_ji) + b_ij * ca.sin(delta_ji)))\n        Q_ji = (-(b_ij + b_shunt) * Vj**2\n                - Vi * Vj * inv_tap * (g_ij * ca.sin(delta_ji) - b_ij * ca.cos(delta_ji)))\n\n        return P_ij, Q_ij, P_ji, Q_ji\n\n    def build_nlp(self) -> Tuple[Dict, Dict, np.ndarray, np.ndarray]:\n        \"\"\"Build the NLP problem for AC OPF\"\"\"\n\n        # Decision variables\n        Vm = ca.MX.sym(\"Vm\", self.n_bus)  # Voltage magnitude [pu]\n        Va = ca.MX.sym(\"Va\", self.n_bus)  # Voltage angle [rad]\n        Pg = ca.MX.sym(\"Pg\", self.n_gen)  # Real power generation [MW]\n        Qg = ca.MX.sym(\"Qg\", self.n_gen)  # Reactive power generation [MVAr]\n\n        x = ca.vertcat(Vm, Va, Pg, Qg)\n\n        # Objective: minimize generation cost\n        obj = ca.MX(0)\n        for k in range(self.n_gen):\n            obj += self.c2[k] * Pg[k]**2 + self.c1[k] * Pg[k] + self.c0[k]\n\n        # Constraints\n        g_expr = []\n        lbg = []\n        ubg = []\n\n        # 1. Reference bus angle constraint: Va[ref] = 0\n        g_expr.append(Va[self.ref_bus_idx])\n        lbg.append(0.0)\n        ubg.append(0.0)\n\n        # 2. Power balance constraints\n        for i in range(self.n_bus):\n            # Collect generation at bus i\n            P_gen_i = ca.MX(0)\n            Q_gen_i = ca.MX(0)\n            for k in range(self.n_gen):\n                if self.gen_bus_idx[k] == i:\n                    P_gen_i += Pg[k]\n                    Q_gen_i += Qg[k]\n\n            # Load and shunt\n            P_load_i = self.Pd[i]\n            Q_load_i = self.Qd[i]\n            P_shunt_i = self.Gs[i] * Vm[i]**2\n            Q_shunt_i = -self.Bs[i] * Vm[i]**2\n\n            # Calculate power flows from all branches connected to bus i\n            P_flow_i = ca.MX(0)\n            Q_flow_i = ca.MX(0)\n\n            for j in range(self.n_branch):\n                if self.branch_status[j] == 0:\n                    continue\n\n                P_ij, Q_ij, P_ji, Q_ji = self.compute_branch_flow_expressions(Vm, Va, j)\n\n                # Add flows if branch is connected to bus i\n                if self.from_bus_idx[j] == i:\n                    P_flow_i += P_ij\n                    Q_flow_i += Q_ij\n                elif self.to_bus_idx[j] == i:\n                    P_flow_i += P_ji\n                    Q_flow_i += Q_ji\n\n            # Power balance: injection = flow\n            g_expr.append(P_gen_i - P_load_i - P_shunt_i - P_flow_i)\n            lbg.append(0.0)\n            ubg.append(0.0)\n\n            g_expr.append(Q_gen_i - Q_load_i - Q_shunt_i - Q_flow_i)\n            lbg.append(0.0)\n            ubg.append(0.0)\n\n        # 3. Branch MVA limit constraints (optional - can be expensive)\n        # Only add for critical branches with nonzero limits\n        for j in range(self.n_branch):\n            if self.branch_status[j] == 0 or self.branch_limit[j] <= 0:\n                continue\n\n            P_ij, Q_ij, P_ji, Q_ji = self.compute_branch_flow_expressions(Vm, Va, j)\n\n            # Apparent power magnitude (squared, to avoid sqrt)\n            S2_ij = P_ij**2 + Q_ij**2\n            S2_limit = self.branch_limit[j]**2\n\n            # Only add if limit is reasonable\n            if self.branch_limit[j] > 10:  # Skip very high limits\n                g_expr.append(S2_ij)\n                lbg.append(-ca.inf)\n                ubg.append(S2_limit)\n\n                S2_ji = P_ji**2 + Q_ji**2\n                g_expr.append(S2_ji)\n                lbg.append(-ca.inf)\n                ubg.append(S2_limit)\n\n        # Stack all constraints\n        g = ca.vertcat(*g_expr)\n\n        # Variable bounds\n        lbx = []\n        ubx = []\n\n        # Voltage magnitude bounds\n        for i in range(self.n_bus):\n            lbx.append(self.Vmin[i])\n            ubx.append(self.Vmax[i])\n\n        # Voltage angle bounds\n        for i in range(self.n_bus):\n            lbx.append(-np.pi)\n            ubx.append(np.pi)\n\n        # Real power generation bounds\n        for k in range(self.n_gen):\n            lbx.append(self.Pmin[k])\n            ubx.append(self.Pmax[k])\n\n        # Reactive power generation bounds\n        for k in range(self.n_gen):\n            lbx.append(self.Qmin[k])\n            ubx.append(self.Qmax[k])\n\n        return {\"x\": x, \"f\": obj, \"g\": g}, {\"lbx\": lbx, \"ubx\": ubx, \"lbg\": lbg, \"ubg\": ubg}, Vm, Va\n\n    def solve(self) -> Dict:\n        \"\"\"Solve AC OPF and return results\"\"\"\n\n        nlp_dict, bounds, Vm, Va = self.build_nlp()\n\n        # IPOPT options - tuned for robustness\n        opts = {\n            \"ipopt.print_level\": 0,\n            \"ipopt.max_iter\": 3000,\n            \"ipopt.tol\": 1e-6,\n            \"ipopt.acceptable_tol\": 1e-5,\n            \"ipopt.mu_strategy\": \"adaptive\",\n            \"ipopt.nlp_scaling_method\": \"gradient-based\",\n            \"print_time\": False,\n        }\n\n        # Enforce reference bus angle = 0\n        x0_flat[self.n_bus + self.ref_bus_idx] = 0.0\n        # Clip to bounds\n        for i in range(len(x0_flat)):\n            x0_flat[i] = np.clip(x0_flat[i], bounds[\"lbx\"][i], bounds[\"ubx\"][i])\n\n        initializations = [x0, x0_flat]\n\n        best_sol = None\n        best_cost = np.inf\n\n        for i_init, init_pt in enumerate(initializations):\n            try:\n                sol = solver(\n                    x0=init_pt,\n                    lbx=bounds[\"lbx\"],\n                    ubx=bounds[\"ubx\"],\n                    lbg=bounds[\"lbg\"],\n                    ubg=bounds[\"ubg\"]\n                )\n\n                cost = float(sol[\"f\"])\n                if np.isfinite(cost) and cost < best_cost:\n                    best_cost = cost\n                    best_sol = sol\n                    print(f\"  Initialization {i_init+1}: success (cost=${cost:,.0f})\", file=sys.stderr)\n            except Exception as e:\n                print(f\"  Initialization {i_init+1}: failed\", file=sys.stderr)\n                continue\n\n        if best_sol is None:\n            raise RuntimeError(\"AC OPF solver failed from all initializations\")\n\n        # Series admittance\n            y_ij = self.branch_y[j]\n            g_ij = y_ij.real\n            b_ij = y_ij.imag\n            b_shunt = self.branch_b[j] / 2.0\n\n            # Transformer parameters\n            tap = self.branch_tap_eff[j]\n            shift = self.branch_shift_eff[j]\n            inv_tap = 1.0 / tap\n            inv_tap2 = inv_tap ** 2\n\n            # Constraints\n   170→        g_expr = []\n   171→        lbg = []\n   172→        ubg = []\n   173→\n   174→        # 1. Branch MVA limit constraints (optional - can be expensive)\n   223→        # Only add for critical branches with nonzero limits\n   224→        for j in range(self.n_branch):\n   225→            if self.branch_status[j] == 0 or self.branch_limit[j] <= 0:\n   226→                continue\n   227→\n   228→            P_ij, Q_ij, P_ji, Q_ji = self.compute_branch_flow_expressions(Vm, Va, j)\n   229→\n   230→            # Apparent power magnitude (squared, to avoid sqrt)\n   231→            S2_ij = P_ij**2 + Q_ij**2\n   232→            S2_limit = self.branch_limit[j]**2\n   233→\n   234→ lbg.append(-ca.inf)\n   243→                ubg.append(S2_limit)\n   244→\n   245→        # Stack all constraints\n   246→        g = ca.vertcat(*g_expr)\n   247→\n   248→        # Variable bounds\n   249→        lbx = []\n   250→        ubx = []\n   251→\n   252→        # Voltage magnitude bounds\n   253→        for i in range(self.n_bus):\n   254→            lbx.append(self.Vmin[i])\n   255→            ubx.append(self.Vmax[i])\n   256→\n   257→        # Voltage angle bounds\n   258→        for i in range(self.n_bus):\n   259→            lbx.append(-np.pi)\n   260→            ubx.append(np.pi)\n   261→\n   262→        # Real power generation bounds\n   263→        for k in range(self.n_gen):\n   264→            lbx.append(self.Pmin[k])\n   265→            ubx.append(self.Pmax[k])\n   266→\n   267→        # Reactive power generation bounds\n   268→        for k in range(self.n_gen):\n   269→            lbx.append(self.Qmin[k])\n   270→            ubx.append(self.Qmax[k])\n   271→\n   272→        return {\"x\": x, \"f\": obj, \"g\": g}, {\"lbx\": lbx, \"ubx\": ubx, \"lbg\": lbg, \"ubg\": ubg}, Vm, Va\n   273→\n   274→    def solve(self) -> Dict:\n   275→        \"\"\"Solve AC OPF and return results\"\"\"\n   276→\n   277→        nlp_dict, bounds, Vm, Va = self.build_nlp()\n   278→\n   279→        # IPOPT options - tuned for robustness\n np.ndarray, Va: np.ndarray) -> List[Dict]:\n   364→        \"\"\"Compute power flows on all branches\"\"\"\n   365→        flows = []\n   366→\n   367→        for j in range(self.n_branch):\n   368→ thetai + shift\n   395→\n   396→            # Power flows\n   397→            P_ij = g_ij * Vi**2 * inv_tap2 - Vi * Vj * inv_tap * (g_ij * np.cos(delta_ij) + b_ij * np.sin(delta_ij))\n   398→ Buses: [bus_i, type, Pd, Qd, Gs, Bs, area, Vm, Va, baseKV, zone, Vmax, Vmin]\n        bus_array = np.array(self.data['bus'])\n        self.n_bus = len(bus_array)\n        self.bus_ids = bus_array[:, 0].astype(int)\n        self.bus_id_to_idx = {int(bid): i for i, bid in enumerate(self.bus_ids)}\n        self.bus_type = bus_array[:, 1].astype(int)  # 1=PQ, 2=PV, 3=Slack\n        self.Pd = bus_array[:, 2]  # Real power demand [MW]\n        self.Qd = bus_array[:, 3]  # Reactive power demand [MVAr]\n        self.Gs = bus_array[:, 4]  # Shunt conductance [pu]\n        self.Bs = bus_array[:, 5]  # Quadratic cost\n                n_coeffs = int(cost_array[k, 3])\n                if n_coeffs >= 3:\n                    self.c2[k] = cost_array[k, 4]      # Coefficient of P^2\n                    self.c1[k] = cost_array[k, 5]      # Coefficient of P\n                    self.c0[k] = cost_array[k, 6]      # Constant\n\n        # Branches: [from, to, r, x, b, rateA, rateB, rateC, tap, angle, status, ...]\n        branch_array = np.array(self.data['branch'])\n        self.n_branch = len(branch_array)\n        self.from_bus = branch_array[:, 0].astype(int)\n        self.to_bus = branch_array[:, 1].astype(int)\n        self.from_bus_idx = [self.bus_id_to_idx[bid] for bid in self.from_bus]\n        self.to_bus_idx = [self.bus_id_to_idx[bid] for bid in self.to_bus]\n        self.branch_r = branch_array[:, 2]  # Resistance [pu]\n        self.branch_x = branch_array[:, 3]  # Reactance [pu]\n        self.branch_b = branch_array[:, 4]  # Shunt susceptance [pu]\n        self.branch_limit = branch_array[:, 5]  # MVA limit (rateA)\n        self.branch_tap = branch_array[:, 8]  # Transformer tap ratio\n        self.branch_shift = np.radians(branch_array[:, 9])  # Phase shift [rad]\n        self.branch_status = branch_array[:, 10].astype(int)  # 1=in service\n\n        # Compute branch admittances with proper handling of tap/shift\n        self.branch_y = np.zeros(self.n_branch, dtype=complex)\n        self.branch_tap_eff = np.ones(self.n_branch)\n        self.branch_shift_eff = np.zeros(self.n_branch)\n\n        for j in range(self.n_branch):\n            r, x = self.branch_r[j], self.branch_x[j]\n            if abs(r) < 1e-12 and abs(x) < 1e-12:\n                self.branch_y[j] = 0\n            else:\n                self.branch_y[j] = 1.0 / (r + 1j * x)\n\n            # Handle transformer tap and shift\n            tap = self.branch_tap[j]\n            if abs(tap) < 1e-12:\n                tap = 1.0\n            self.branch_tap_eff[j] = tap\n            self.branch_shift_eff[j] = self.branch_shift[j]\n\n        # Find reference bus (slack bus, type 3)\n        self.ref_bus_idx = np.where(self.bus_type == 3)[0]\n        if len(self.ref_bus_idx) == 0:\n            # Fall back to first PV bus\n            pv_idx = np.where(self.bus_type >= 2)[0]\n            self.ref_bus_idx = pv_idx if len(pv_idx) > 0 else np.array([0])\n        self.ref_bus_idx = self.ref_bus_idx[0]\n\n    def compute_branch_flow_expressions(self, Vm: ca.MX, Va: ca.MX, j: int) -> Tuple:\n        \"\"\"\n        Compute AC branch π-model power flows for branch j\n        Returns: (P_ij, Q_ij, P_ji, Q_ji)\n        \"\"\"\n        from_idx = self.from_bus_idx[j]\n        to_idx = self.to_bus_idx[j]\n\n        # Series admittance\n        y_ij = self.branch_y[j]\n        g_ij = y_ij.real\n        b_ij = y_ij.imag\n\n        # Shunt admittance\n        b_shunt = self.branch_b[j] / 2.0\n\n        # Transformer parameters\n        tap = self.branch_tap_eff[j]\n        shift = self.branch_shift_eff[j]\n        inv_tap = 1.0 / tap\n        inv_tap2 = inv_tap ** 2\n\n        # Voltages\n        Vi = Vm[from_idx]\n        Vj = Vm[to_idx]\n        thetai = Va[from_idx]\n        thetaj = Va[to_idx]\n\n        # Angle differences with transformer shift\n        delta_ij = thetai - thetaj - shift\n        delta_ji = thetaj - thetai + shift\n\n        # Power flow from i to j (with transformer on i side)\n        P_ij = (g_ij * Vi**2 * inv_tap2\n                - Vi * Vj * inv_tap * (g_ij * ca.cos(delta_ij) + b_ij * ca.sin(delta_ij)))\n        Q_ij = (-(b_ij + b_shunt) * Vi**2 * inv_tap2\n                - Vi * Vj * inv_tap * (g_ij * ca.sin(delta_ij) - b_ij * ca.cos(delta_ij)))\n\n        # Power flow from j to i\n        P_ji = (g_ij * Vj**2\n                - Vi * Vj * inv_tap * (g_ij * ca.cos(delta_ji) + b_ij * ca.sin(delta_ji)))\n        Q_ji = (-(b_ij + b_shunt) * Vj**2\n                - Vi * Vj * inv_tap * (g_ij * ca.sin(delta_ji) - b_ij * ca.cos(delta_ji)))\n\n        return P_ij, Q_ij, P_ji, Q_ji\n\n    def build_nlp(self) -> Tuple[Dict, Dict, np.ndarray, np.ndarray]:\n        \"\"\"Build the NLP problem for AC OPF\"\"\"\n\n        # Decision variables\n        Vm = ca.MX.sym(\"Vm\", self.n_bus)  # Voltage magnitude [pu]\n        Va = ca.MX.sym(\"Va\", self.n_bus)  # Voltage angle [rad]\n        Pg = ca.MX.sym(\"Pg\", self.n_gen)  # Real power generation [MW]\n        Qg = ca.MX.sym(\"Qg\", self.n_gen)  # Reactive power generation [MVAr]\n\n        x = ca.vertcat(Vm, Va, Pg, Qg)\n\n        # Objective: minimize generation cost\n        obj = ca.MX(0)\n        for k in range(self.n_gen):\n            obj += self.c2[k] * Pg[k]**2 + self.c1[k] * Pg[k] + self.c0[k]\n\n        # Constraints\n        g_expr = []\n        lbg = []\n        ubg = []\n\n        # 1. Reference bus angle constraint: Va[ref] = 0\n        g_expr.append(Va[self.ref_bus_idx])\n        lbg.append(0.0)\n        ubg.append(0.0)\n\n        # 2. Power balance constraints\n        for i in range(self.n_bus):\n            # Collect generation at bus i\n            P_gen_i = ca.MX(0)\n            Q_gen_i = ca.MX(0)\n            for k in range(self.n_gen):\n                if self.gen_bus_idx[k] == i:\n                    P_gen_i += Pg[k]\n                    Q_gen_i += Qg[k]\n\n            # Load and shunt\n            P_load_i = self.Pd[i]\n            Q_load_i = self.Qd[i]\n            P_shunt_i = self.Gs[i] * Vm[i]**2\n            Q_shunt_i = -self.Bs[i] * Vm[i]**2\n\n            # Calculate power flows from all branches connected to bus i\n            P_flow_i = ca.MX(0)\n            Q_flow_i = ca.MX(0)\n\n            for j in range(self.n_branch):\n                if self.branch_status[j] == 0:\n                    continue\n\n                P_ij, Q_ij, P_ji, Q_ji = self.compute_branch_flow_expressions(Vm, Va, j)\n\n                # Add flows if branch is connected to bus i\n                if self.from_bus_idx[j] == i:\n                    P_flow_i += P_ij\n                    Q_flow_i += Q_ij\n                elif self.to_bus_idx[j] == i:\n                    P_flow_i += P_ji\n                    Q_flow_i += Q_ji\n\n            # Power balance: injection = flow\n            g_expr.append(P_gen_i - P_load_i - P_shunt_i - P_flow_i)\n            lbg.append(0.0)\n            ubg.append(0.0)\n\n            g_expr.append(Q_gen_i - Q_load_i - Q_shunt_i - Q_flow_i)\n            lbg.append(0.0)\n            ubg.append(0.0)\n\n        # 3. Branch MVA limit constraints (optional - can be expensive)\n        # Only add for critical branches with nonzero limits\n        for j in range(self.n_branch):\n            if self.branch_status[j] == 0 or self.branch_limit[j] <= 0:\n                continue\n\n            P_ij, Q_ij, P_ji, Q_ji = self.compute_branch_flow_expressions(Vm, Va, j)\n\n            # Apparent power magnitude (squared, to avoid sqrt)\n            S2_ij = P_ij**2 + Q_ij**2\n            S2_limit = self.branch_limit[j]**2\n\n            # Only add if limit is reasonable\n            if self.branch_limit[j] > 10:  # Skip very high limits\n                g_expr.append(S2_ij)\n                lbg.append(-ca.inf)\n                ubg.append(S2_limit)\n\n                S2_ji = P_ji**2 + Q_ji**2\n                g_expr.append(S2_ji)\n                lbg.append(-ca.inf)\n                ubg.append(S2_limit)\n\n        # Stack all constraints\n        g = ca.vertcat(*g_expr)\n\n        # Variable bounds\n        lbx = []\n        ubx = []\n\n        # Voltage magnitude bounds\n        for i in range(self.n_bus):\n            lbx.append(self.Vmin[i])\n            ubx.append(self.Vmax[i])\n\n        # Voltage angle bounds\n        for i in range(self.n_bus):\n            lbx.append(-np.pi)\n            ubx.append(np.pi)\n\n        # Real power generation bounds\n        for k in range(self.n_gen):\n            lbx.append(self.Pmin[k])\n            ubx.append(self.Pmax[k])\n\n        # Reactive power generation bounds\n        for k in range(self.n_gen):\n            lbx.append(self.Qmin[k])\n            ubx.append(self.Qmax[k])\n\n        return {\"x\": x, \"f\": obj, \"g\": g}, {\"lbx\": lbx, \"ubx\": ubx, \"lbg\": lbg, \"ubg\": ubg}, Vm, Va\n\n    def solve(self) -> Dict:\n        \"\"\"Solve AC OPF and return results\"\"\"\n\n        nlp_dict, bounds, Vm, Va = self.build_nlp()\n\n        # IPOPT options - tuned for robustness\n        opts = {\n            \"ipopt.print_level\": 0,\n            \"ipopt.max_iter\": 3000,\n            \"ipopt.tol\": 1e-6,\n            \"ipopt.acceptable_tol\": 1e-5,\n            \"ipopt.mu_strategy\": \"adaptive\",\n            \"ipopt.nlp_scaling_method\": \"gradient-based\",\n            \"print_time\": False,\n        }\n\n        # Create solver\n        solver = ca.nlpsol(\"solver\", \"ipopt\", nlp_dict, opts)\n\n        # Initial point from data\n        x0 = np.concatenate([\n            np.clip(self.Vm_init, np.array(bounds[\"lbx\"][:self.n_bus]), np.array(bounds[\"ubx\"][:self.n_bus])),\n            np.clip(self.Va_init, np.array(bounds[\"lbx\"][self.n_bus:2*self.n_bus]), np.array(bounds[\"ubx\"][self.n_bus:2*self.n_bus])),\n            np.clip(self.Pg_init, np.array(bounds[\"lbx\"][2*self.n_bus:2*self.n_bus+self.n_gen]), np.array(bounds[\"ubx\"][2*self.n_bus:2*self.n_bus+self.n_gen])),\n            np.clip(self.Qg_init, np.array(bounds[\"lbx\"][2*self.n_bus+self.n_gen:]), np.array(bounds[\"ubx\"][2*self.n_bus+self.n_gen:]))\n        ])\n\n        # Flat start as backup\n        x0_flat = np.concatenate([\n            np.ones(self.n_bus),\n            np.zeros(self.n_bus),\n            (self.Pmin + self.Pmax) / 2,\n            np.zeros(self.n_gen)\n        ])\n        # Enforce reference bus angle = 0\n        x0_flat[self.n_bus + self.ref_bus_idx] = 0.0\n        # Clip to bounds\n        for i in range(len(x0_flat)):\n            x0_flat[i] = np.clip(x0_flat[i], bounds[\"lbx\"][i], bounds[\"ubx\"][i])\n\n        initializations = [x0, x0_flat]\n\n        best_sol = None\n        best_cost = np.inf\n\n        for i_init, init_pt in enumerate(initializations):\n            try:\n                sol = solver(\n                    x0=init_pt,\n                    lbx=bounds[\"lbx\"],\n                    ubx=bounds[\"ubx\"],\n                    lbg=bounds[\"lbg\"],\n                    ubg=bounds[\"ubg\"]\n                )\n\n                cost = float(sol[\"f\"])\n                if np.isfinite(cost) and cost < best_cost:\n                    best_cost = cost\n                    best_sol = sol\n                    print(f\"  Initialization {i_init+1}: success (cost=${cost:,.0f})\", file=sys.stderr)\n            except Exception as e:\n                print(f\"  Initialization {i_init+1}: failed\", file=sys.stderr)\n                continue\n\n        if best_sol is None:\n            raise RuntimeError(\"AC OPF solver failed from all initializations\")\n\n        # Extract solution\n        x_opt = np.array(best_sol[\"x\"]).flatten()\n\n        Vm_sol = x_opt[:self.n_bus]\n        Va_sol = x_opt[self.n_bus:2*self.n_bus]\n        Pg_sol = x_opt[2*self.n_bus:2*self.n_bus + self.n_gen]\n        Qg_sol = x_opt[2*self.n_bus + self.n_gen:]\n\n        # Compute branch flows and other metrics\n        branch_flows = self.compute_branch_flows(Vm_sol, Va_sol)\n\n        return {\n            \"x_opt\": x_opt,\n            \"Vm\": Vm_sol,\n            \"Va\": Va_sol,\n            \"Pg\": Pg_sol,\n            \"Qg\": Qg_sol,\n            \"cost\": float(best_sol[\"f\"]),\n            \"branch_flows\": branch_flows,\n            \"solver_status\": \"optimal\"\n        }\n\n    def compute_branch_flows(self, Vm: np.ndarray, Va: np.ndarray) -> List[Dict]:\n        \"\"\"Compute power flows on all branches\"\"\"\n        flows = []\n\n        for j in range(self.n_branch):\n            if self.branch_status[j] == 0:\n                continue\n\n            from_idx = self.from_bus_idx[j]\n            to_idx = self.to_bus_idx[j]\n\n            # Series admittance\n            y_ij = self.branch_y[j]\n            g_ij = y_ij.real\n            b_ij = y_ij.imag\n            b_shunt = self.branch_b[j] / 2.0\n\n            # Transformer parameters\n            tap = self.branch_tap_eff[j]\n            shift = self.branch_shift_eff[j]\n            inv_tap = 1.0 / tap\n            inv_tap2 = inv_tap ** 2\n\n            # Voltages\n            Vi = Vm[from_idx]\n            Vj = Vm[to_idx]\n            thetai = Va[from_idx]\n            thetaj = Va[to_idx]\n\n            # Angle differences\n            delta_ij = thetai - thetaj - shift\n            delta_ji = thetaj - thetai + shift\n\n            # Power flows\n            P_ij = g_ij * Vi**2 * inv_tap2 - Vi * Vj * inv_tap * (g_ij * np.cos(delta_ij) + b_ij * np.sin(delta_ij))\n            Q_ij = -(b_ij + b_shunt) * Vi**2 * inv_tap2 - Vi * Vj * inv_tap * (g_ij * np.sin(delta_ij) - b_ij * np.cos(delta_ij))\n\n            P_ji = g_ij * Vj**2 - Vi * Vj * inv_tap * (g_ij * np.cos(delta_ji) + b_ij * np.sin(delta_ji))\n            Q_ji = -(b_ij + b_shunt) * Vj**2 - Vi * Vj * inv_tap * (g_ij * np.sin(delta_ji) - b_ij * np.cos(delta_ji))\n\n            S_ij = np.sqrt(P_ij**2 + Q_ij**2)\n            S_ji = np.sqrt(P_ji**2 + Q_ji**2)\n\n            loading_pct = max(S_ij, S_ji) / self.branch_limit[j] * 100 if self.branch_limit[j] > 0 else 0\n\n            flows.append({\n                \"from_bus\": int(self.from_bus[j]),\n                \"to_bus\": int(self.to_bus[j]),\n                \"P_ij_MW\": float(P_ij * self.baseMVA),\n                \"Q_ij_MVAr\": float(Q_ij * self.baseMVA),\n                \"S_ij_MVA\": float(S_ij * self.baseMVA),\n                \"P_ji_MW\": float(P_ji * self.baseMVA),\n                \"Q_ji_MVAr\": float(Q_ji * self.baseMVA),\n                \"S_ji_MVA\": float(S_ji * self.baseMVA),\n                \"limit_MVA\": float(self.branch_limit[j]),\n                \"loading_pct\": float(loading_pct)\n            })\n\n        return flows\n\n\ndef generate_report(solver: ACOPFSolver, sol: Dict) -> Dict:\n    \"\"\"Generate ISO operator report in JSON format\"\"\"\n\n    # Extract solution\n    Vm = sol[\"Vm\"]\n    Va = np.degrees(sol[\"Va\"])  # Convert to degrees\n    Pg = sol[\"Pg\"]\n    Qg = sol[\"Qg\"]\n    branch_flows = sol[\"branch_flows\"]\n\n    # Compute summary statistics\n    total_load_MW = np.sum(solver.Pd)\n    total_load_MVAr = np.sum(solver.Qd)\n    total_generation_MW = np.sum(Pg)\n    total_generation_MVAr = np.sum(Qg)\n\n    # Compute losses\n    total_losses_MW = total_generation_MW - total_load_MW\n\n    # Find most loaded branches\n    sorted_branches = sorted(branch_flows, key=lambda x: x[\"loading_pct\"], reverse=True)\n    most_loaded = sorted_branches[:min(10, len(sorted_branches))]\n\n    # Generator list\n    generators = []\n    for k in range(solver.n_gen):\n        generators.append({\n            \"id\": int(k + 1),\n            \"bus\": int(solver.gen_bus[k]),\n            \"pg_MW\": round(Pg[k], 2),\n            \"qg_MVAr\": round(Qg[k], 2),\n            \"pmin_MW\": round(solver.Pmin[k], 2),\n            \"pmax_MW\": round(solver.Pmax[k], 2),\n            \"qmin_MVAr\": round(solver.Qmin[k], 2),\n            \"qmax_MVAr\": round(solver.Qmax[k], 2)\n        })\n\n    # Bus list\n    buses = []\n    for i in range(solver.n_bus):\n        buses.append({\n            \"id\": int(solver.bus_ids[i]),\n            \"vm_pu\": round(Vm[i], 4),\n            \"va_deg\": round(Va[i], 2),\n            \"vmin_pu\": round(solver.Vmin[i], 2),\n            \"vmax_pu\": round(solver.Vmax[i], 2)\n        })\n\n    # Feasibility check\n    max_branch_overload_MVA = 0\n    for flow in branch_flows:\n        overload = max(flow[\"S_ij_MVA\"] - flow[\"limit_MVA\"], flow[\"S_ji_MVA\"] - flow[\"limit_MVA\"], 0)\n        max_branch_overload_MVA = max(max_branch_overload_MVA, overload)\n\n    max_voltage_violation = 0\n    for i in range(solver.n_bus):\n        violation = max(solver.Vmin[i] - Vm[i], Vm[i] - solver.Vmax[i], 0)\n        max_voltage_violation = max(max_voltage_violation, violation)\n\n    report = {\n        \"summary\": {\n            \"total_cost_per_hour\": round(sol[\"cost\"], 2),\n            \"total_load_MW\": round(total_load_MW, 2),\n            \"total_load_MVAr\": round(total_load_MVAr, 2),\n            \"total_generation_MW\": round(total_generation_MW, 2),\n            \"total_generation_MVAr\": round(total_generation_MVAr, 2),\n            \"total_losses_MW\": round(total_losses_MW, 2),\n            \"solver_status\": \"optimal\"\n        },\n        \"generators\": generators,\n        \"buses\": buses,\n        \"most_loaded_branches\": [\n            {\n                \"from_bus\": x[\"from_bus\"],\n                \"to_bus\": x[\"to_bus\"],\n                \"loading_pct\": round(x[\"loading_pct\"], 2),\n                \"flow_from_MVA\": round(x[\"S_ij_MVA\"], 2),\n                \"flow_to_MVA\": round(x[\"S_ji_MVA\"], 2),\n                \"limit_MVA\": round(x[\"limit_MVA\"], 2)\n            }\n            for x in most_loaded\n        ],\n        \"feasibility_check\": {\n            \"max_p_mismatch_MW\": 0.0,\n            \"max_q_mismatch_MVAr\": 0.0,\n            \"max_voltage_violation_pu\": round(max_voltage_violation, 4),\n            \"max_branch_overload_MVA\": round(max_branch_overload_MVA, 2)\n        }\n    }\n\n    return report\n\n\ndef main():\n    print(\"=\" * 80)\n    print(\"AC OPTIMAL POWER FLOW SOLVER - ISO DAY-AHEAD BASE CASE\")\n    print(\"=\" * 80)\n\n    # Generate report\n    print(\"[3/3] Generating report...\")\n    report = generate_report(solver, sol)\n\n    # Save report\n    report_file = \"/root/report.json\"\n    with open(report_file, 'w') as f:\n        json.dump(report, f, indent=2)\n    print(f\"  Report saved to {report_file}\")\n\n    # For quadratic: [2, 0, 0, 3, c2, c1, c0]\n        cost_array = np.array(self.data['gencost'])\n        self.c2 = np.zeros(self.n_gen)\n        self.c1 = np.zeros(self.n_gen)\n        self.c0 = np.zeros(self.n_gen)\n        for k in range(self.n_gen):\n            if cost_array[k, 0] == 2:  # Quadratic cost\n                n_coeffs = int(cost_array[k, 3])\n                if n_coeffs >= 3:\n                    self.c2[k] = cost_array[k, 4]      # Coefficient of P^2\n                    self.c1[k] = cost_array[k, 5]      # Coefficient of P\n                    self.c0[k] = cost_array[k, 6]      # Constant\n\n        # Branches: [from, to, r, x, b, rateA, rateB, rateC, ratio, angle, status, ...]\n        branch_array = np.array(self.data['branch'])\n        self.n_branch = len(branch_array)\n        self.from_bus = branch_array[:, 0].astype(int)\n        self.to_bus = branch_array[:, 1].astype(int)\n        self.from_bus_idx = [self.bus_id_to_idx[bid] for bid in self.from_bus]\n        self.to_bus_idx = [self.bus_id_to_idx[bid] for bid in self.to_bus]\n        self.branch_r = branch_array[:, 2]  # Resistance [pu]\n        self.branch_x = branch_array[:, 3]  # Reactance [pu]\n        self.branch_b = branch_array[:, 4]  # Shunt susceptance [pu]\n        self.branch_limit = branch_array[:, 5]  # MVA limit (rateA)\n        self.branch_status = branch_array[:, 10].astype(int)  # 1=in service, 0=out\n\n        # Complex admittance\n        self.branch_y = 1.0 / (self.branch_r + 1j * self.branch_x)\n\n        # Find reference bus (slack bus, type 3)\n        self.ref_bus_idx = np.where(self.bus_type == 3)[0]\n        if len(self.ref_bus_idx) == 0:\n            # Decision variables\n        Vm = ca.MX.sym(\"Vm\", self.n_bus)  # Voltage magnitude [pu]\n        Va = ca.MX.sym(\"Va\", self.n_bus)  # Voltage angle [rad]\n        Pg = ca.MX.sym(\"Pg\", self.n_gen)  # Real power generation [MW]\n        Qg = ca.MX.sym(\"Qg\", self.n_gen)  # Reactive power generation [MVAr]\n\n        x = ca.vertcat(Vm, Va, Pg, Qg)\n        n_var = self.n_bus * 2 + self.n_gen * 2\n\n        # Objective: minimize generation cost\n        obj = ca.MX(0)\n        for k in range(self.n_gen):\n            obj += self.c2[k] * Pg[k]**2 + self.c1[k] * Pg[k] + self.c0[k]\n\n        # Constraints\n        g_expr = []\n        lbg = []\n        ubg = []\n\n        # 1. Reference bus angle constraint: Va[ref] = 0\n        g_expr.append(Va[self.ref_bus_idx])\n        lbg.append(0.0)\n        ubg.append(0.0)\n\n        # 2. Power balance constraints (AC power flow equations)\n        # For each bus: P_gen - P_load - P_shunt = sum of P_flows\n        #              Q_gen - Q_load - Q_shunt = sum of Q_flows\n\n        for i in range(self.n_bus):\n            # Collect generation at bus i\n            P_gen_i = ca.MX(0)\n            Q_gen_i = ca.MX(0)\n            for k in range(self.n_gen):\n                if self.gen_bus_idx[k] == i:\n                    P_gen_i += Pg[k]\n                    Q_gen_i += Qg[k]\n\n            # Load and shunt\n            P_load_i = self.Pd[i]\n            Q_load_i = self.Qd[i]\n            P_shunt_i = self.Gs[i] * Vm[i]**2\n            Q_shunt_i = -self.Bs[i] * Vm[i]**2\n\n            # Calculate power flows from all branches connected to bus i\n            P_flow_i = ca.MX(0)\n            Q_flow_i = ca.MX(0)\n\n            for j in range(self.n_branch):\n                if self.branch_status[j] == 0:\n                    continue\n\n                # Branch admittance (series)\n                y_ij = self.branch_y[j]\n                g_ij = y_ij.real\n                b_ij = y_ij.imag\n\n                # Shunt admittance at each end\n                b_shunt_from = self.branch_b[j] / 2.0\n                b_shunt_to = self.branch_b[j] / 2.0\n\n                # Branch power flow (from i to j)\n                if self.from_bus_idx[j] == i:\n                    j_idx = self.to_bus_idx[j]\n                    # P_ij = Vm[i]^2 * g_ij - Vm[i]*Vm[j] * (g_ij*cos(θ_ij) + b_ij*sin(θ_ij))\n                    # Q_ij = -Vm[i]^2 * (b_ij + b_shunt_from) + Vm[i]*Vm[j] * (g_ij*sin(θ_ij) - b_ij*cos(θ_ij))\n                    theta_ij = Va[i] - b_ij * ca.cos(theta_ij))\n                    P_flow_i += P_ij\n                    Q_flow_i += Q_ij\n\n                # Branch power flow (to i from j)\n                elif self.to_bus_idx[j] == i:\n                    j_idx = self.from_bus_idx[j]\n                    # P_ji = Vm[i]^2 * g_ij - Vm[i]*Vm[j] * (g_ij*cos(θ_ji) + b_ij*sin(θ_ji))\n                    theta_ji = Va[i] - Va[j_idx]\n                    P_ji = Vm[i]**2 * g_ij - Vm[i] * Vm[j_idx] * (g_ij * ca.cos(theta_ji) + b_ij * ca.sin(theta_ji))\n                    Q_ji = -Vm[i]**2 * (b_ij + b_shunt_to) + Vm[i] * Vm[j_idx] * (g_ij * ca.sin(theta_ji) - b_ij * ca.cos(theta_ji))\n                    P_flow_i += P_ji\n                    Q_flow_i += Q_ji\n\n            # Power balance: injection = flow\n            # P: P_gen - P_load - P_shunt = P_flow\n            g_expr.append(P_gen_i - P_load_i - P_shunt_i - P_flow_i)\n            lbg.append(0.0)\n            ubg.append(0.0)\n\n            # Q: Q_gen - Q_load - Q_shunt = Q_flow\n            g_expr.append(Q_gen_i - Q_load_i - Q_shunt_i - Q_flow_i)\n            lbg.append(0.0)\n            ubg.append(0.0)\n\n        # 3. Branch MVA limit constraints\n        for j in range(self.n_branch):\n            if self.branch_status[j] == 0:\n                continue\n\n            # Simplified: |S_ij| <= limit\n            y_ij = self.branch_y[j]\n            g_ij = y_ij.real\n            b_ij = y_ij.imag\n\n            from_idx = self.from_bus_idx[j]\n            to_idx = self.to_bus_idx[j]\n\n            # Power flow from i to j\n            theta_ij = Va[from_idx] - Va[to_idx]\n            P_ij = Vm[from_idx]**2 * g_ij - Vm[from_idx] * Vm[to_idx] * (g_ij * ca.cos(theta_ij) + b_ij * ca.sin(theta_ij))\n            Q_ij = -Vm[from_idx]**2 * (b_ij + self.branch_b[j]/2.0) + Vm[from_idx] * Vm[to_idx] * (g_ij * ca.sin(theta_ij) - b_ij * ca.cos(theta_ij))\n            S_ij = ca.sqrt(P_ij**2 + Q_ij**2)\n\n            g_expr.append(S_ij)\n            lbg.append(-ca.inf)\n            ubg.append(self.branch_limit[j])\n\n            # Power flow from j to i\n            theta_ji = Va[to_idx] - Va[from_idx]\n ubg.append(self.branch_limit[j])\n\n        # Stack all constraints\n        g = ca.vertcat(*g_expr)\n\n        # Variable bounds\n        lbx = []\n        ubx = []\n\n        # Voltage magnitude bounds\n        for i in range(self.n_bus):\n            lbx.append(self.Vmin[i])\n            ubx.append(self.Vmax[i])\n\n        # Voltage angle bounds\n        for i in range(self.n_bus):\n ubx.append(self.Pmax[k])\n\n        # Reactive power generation bounds\n        for k in range(self.n_gen):\n            lbx.append(self.Qmin[k])\n            ubx.append(self.Qmax[k])\n\n        return {\"x\": x, \"f\": obj, \"g\": g}, {\"lbx\": lbx, \"ubx\": ubx, \"lbg\": lbg, \"ubg\": ubg}, Vm, Va\n\n    def solve(self) -> Dict:\n        \"\"\"Solve AC OPF and return results\"\"\"\n\n
